Gradient-Guided Action Refinement via openpi train_loop hooks.\\n\\nExtends A1 (SDF auxiliary task) with gradient-guided action refinement:\\nuses dSDF/d(suffix features) to compute a \\\"collision-safe\\\" action target\\nand trains the model to output it directly.\\n\\nTwo-phase training:\\n  Phase 1 (0 -> warmup_phase_steps): Freeze backbone + LoRA. Train SDF decoder +\\n      EE extractor only (~658K params). SDF must be accurate before refinement.\\n  Phase 2 (warmup_phase_steps -> end): Unfreeze LoRA (~33.5M). Enable gradient\\n      refinement. Train with full loss:\\n      Loss = action_loss + lambda_sdf * sdf_loss + lambda_ee * ee_loss + lambda_refine * refine_loss\\n\\nKey difference from A1:\\n  - A1: SDF is an auxiliary loss that improves feature quality\\n  - A3: SDF gradient explicitly shifts action predictions away from collision\\n\\nDependencies:\\n  - openpi train_pytorch.py (TrainHooks, train_loop)\\n  - colliforce/sdf_decoder.py (SDFDecoder, compute_sdf_loss)\\n  - colliforce/ee_extractor.py (EEPositionExtractor)\\n  - colliforce/sdf_gradient_refiner.py (SDFGradientRefiner)\\n  - SDF GT npz (query_points + sdf_values)\\n\\nUsage:\\n    CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES=3 python scripts/train_a3_hook.py --scene spatial_bowl --dry_run\\n    CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES=3 python scripts/train_a3_hook.py --scene spatial_bowl --seed 0\\n\\\"\\\"\\\"\\nimport argparse\\nimport logging\\nimport os\\nimport sys\\n\\nimport numpy as np\\nimport torch\\nimport torch.nn as nn\\nimport torch.nn.functional as ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\\n# Manual LoRA -- identical to A1 (PEFT not installed)\\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\\n\\nclass LoRALinear(nn.Module):\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Drop-in nn.Linear replacement with low-rank adapters.\\n    Base weight frozen; only lora_A/lora_B are trainable.\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n\\n    def __init__(self, base: nn.Linear, rank: int = 16, alpha: float = 16.0):\\n        super().__init__()\\n        self.base = base\\n        for p in self.base.parameters():\\n            p.requires_grad = False\\n        in_f, out_f = base.in_features, base.out_features\\n        dev = base.weight.device\\n        self.lora_A = nn.Parameter(torch.randn(in_f, rank, device=dev) * 0.01)\\n        self.lora_B = nn.Parameter(torch.zeros(rank, out_f, device=dev))\\n        self.scaling = alpha / rank\\n\\n    @property\\n    def weight(self):\\n        return self.base.weight\\n\\n    @property\\n    def bias(self):\\n        return self.base.bias\\n\\n    def forward(self, x):\\n        base_out = self.base(x.to(self.base.weight.dtype))\\n        lora_out = (x.float() @ self.lora_A.float() @ self.lora_B.float()) * self.scaling\\n        return base_out + lora_out.to(base_out.dtype)\\n\\n\\ndef {"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_013GJZotAAuf2ftsW7XBMpAC","type":"tool_result","content":"{\"result\":\"{\\\"success\\\": true, \\\"data\\\": {\\\"stdout\\\": \\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\nEvaluate a VLA model on SafeLIBERO benchmark.\\\\nSupports three model types:\\\\n  - baseline: Standard openpi pi0 LoRA fine-tune\\\\n  - a1: pi0 + SDF decoder + EE
